Based on my exploration of the ingestion/ directory, here's a comprehensive summary of the existing Cloudflare Workers pattern:

## Bindings and Configuration

**Active Bindings:**
- **D1 Database**: Named binding "DB" connected to the "raw" database (ID: `30fb49b7-8321-4792-8706-06c9205ce026`)
- **Environment Variables**: GITHUB_USERNAME, OURA_REDIRECT_URI, WITHINGS_REDIRECT_URI
- **Secrets** (commented in wrangler.jsonc): GITHUB_TOKEN, OURA_CLIENT_ID, OURA_CLIENT_SECRET, LINEAR_API_KEY, WITHINGS_CLIENT_ID, WITHINGS_CLIENT_SECRET

**Observability**: Workers Observability enabled with full sampling (head_sampling_rate: 1) for comprehensive logging and tracing.

## Worker Structure

The worker uses a **service-based modular architecture**:

```
ingestion (main app)
├── /github → github service
├── /oura → oura service
├── /linear → linear service
└── /withings → withings service
```

**Core Endpoints:**
- `GET /` - API documentation with all available endpoints
- `POST /sync` - Orchestrates synchronization across all services with individual error handling
- `GET /health` - Health check

**Scheduling (Cron Triggers):**
- `0 0 * * *` - GitHub sync (daily at 00:00 UTC)
- `0 */12 * * *` - Oura & Withings sync (every 12 hours)
- `0 */6 * * *` - Linear sync (every 6 hours)

## Framework and Tooling

| Tool | Purpose | Version |
|------|---------|---------|
| **Hono** | Web framework (production only) | ^4.11.7 |
| **TypeScript** | Type-safe development | ^5.7.0 |
| **Biome** | Linting & formatting | ^2.3.13 |
| **Vitest** | Testing framework | ^3.0.0 |
| **Wrangler** | Cloudflare CLI | ^4.4.0 |
| **@cloudflare/workers-types** | Type definitions | ^4.20250124.0 |

## Patterns to Follow for Consistency

**1. Service Routing Pattern:**
Each service (github, oura, linear, withings) is imported with a `runSync` export:
```typescript
import service, { runSync as runServiceSync } from "./services/service-name";
app.route("/service-path", service);
```

**2. Code Formatting (Biome):**
- 2-space indentation
- Double quotes for strings
- Always semicolons
- 100-character line width
- ES5 trailing commas
- Auto-organize imports enabled

**3. Error Handling Pattern:**
Both `/sync` endpoint and scheduled handler wrap service calls in try-catch blocks, logging errors and returning structured results:
```typescript
try {
  results.push(await runServiceSync(env));
} catch (e) {
  results.push({
    service: "name",
    success: false,
    message: e instanceof Error ? e.message : "Unknown error",
  });
}
```

**4. Type Safety:**
- Use TypeScript with full type checking (typecheck script available)
- Bindings typed through Env interface (defined in src/types.ts)
- Response types consistent across services via SyncResult type

**5. Development Scripts:**
```bash
npm run dev          # Local development with wrangler
npm run deploy       # Production deployment with minification
npm run test:run     # Run tests (Vitest)
npm run lint:fix     # Fix linting issues
npm run format       # Format code with Biome
npm run check:fix    # Combined lint + format
```

**6. Scheduled Handler Pattern:**
- Check `event.cron` to determine which schedule triggered
- Handle each schedule's services independently
- Log completion/errors to stdout for Workers Observability

This pattern establishes a clean, scalable foundation where new services can be easily added following the same structure and conventions.

> AGENT

方針が固まりました。アプローチを提案します。

---

## アプローチ比較

### A. 公式 `@modelcontextprotocol/sdk` を使う（推奨）

MCP公式のTypeScript SDKを使い、Workers上でStreamable HTTPトランスポートを実装。

**メリット:**
- プロトコル準拠が保証される
- ツール定義・バリデーション・エラーハンドリングがSDKで処理される
- メンテナンスコストが低い

**デメリット:**
- SDK がWorkers環境で動くか確認が必要（Node.js依存の部分がある可能性）

### B. 自前でJSON-RPC 2.0を実装

MCPプロトコル仕様に従って、JSON-RPCのリクエスト/レスポンスを自前で処理。

**メリット:**
- Workers環境での完全な制御
- 外部依存が最小限

**デメリット:**
- プロトコル準拠の実装が大変（バリデーション、エラーコード等）
- メンテナンスコストが高い

### C. Cloudflare公式の `workers-mcp` パッケージを使う

Cloudflareが提供するWorkers向けMCPラッパーを使う。

**メリット:**
- Workers環境に最適化済み
- セットアップが最も簡単

**デメリット:**
- Cloudflare独自の抽象化で、MCP仕様の理解が薄くなる
- カスタマイズ性が限られる可能性

では設計を提示します。

---

## 設計: mcp-cloudflare (Phase 1 - D1)

### アーキテクチャ

```
Claude Desktop / Claude Code
  ↓ HTTP POST (Streamable HTTP)
Cloudflare Worker (mcp-server/)
  ├── Hono ルーティング
  ├── MCP SDK (McpServer)
  │   ├── tools/d1-query      → D1 SQL実行
  │   ├── tools/d1-list-tables → テーブル一覧取得
  │   └── tools/d1-describe   → スキーマ確認
  └── D1 Binding ("DB")
```

### ディレクトリ構成

```
mcp-server/
├── src/
│   ├── index.ts          # Honoエントリ + MCPトランスポート
│   ├── types.ts          # 型定義 (Env, Bindings)
│   └── tools/
│       └── d1.ts         # D1ツール定義 (query, list-tables, describe)
├── wrangler.jsonc        # Workers設定 (D1バインディング)
├── package.json          # pnpm依存
├── tsconfig.json
├── biome.json            # ingestionと同じ設定
└── vitest.config.ts
```

### エンドポイント

| パス | メソッド | 説明 |
|------|---------|------|
| `/health` | GET | ヘルスチェック |
| `/mcp` | POST | MCP Streamable HTTPエンドポイント |

### D1ツール定義

| ツール名 | 入力パラメータ | 説明 |
|----------|--------------|------|
| `d1-query` | `sql: string`, `params?: any[]` | SQLクエリ実行（SELECT/INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE） |
| `d1-list-tables` | なし | D1内のテーブル一覧取得 |
| `d1-describe` | `table: string` | 指定テーブルのスキーマ（カラム情報）取得 |

### 認証

Worker Secretsで `MCP_AUTH_TOKEN` を管理。リクエストの `Authorization: Bearer <token>` ヘッダーで認証。

---

  "content": "# MCP Cloudflare Server - Phase 1: D1 Design\n\n## Overview\n\nCloudflare D1 を操作できる MCP サーバーを TypeScript/Hono で実装する。\nClaude Desktop や Claude Code から自然言語で D1 データベースを操作できるようになる。\n\nLinear Issue: [TA-216](https://linear.app/ta93abe/issue/TA-216)\n\n## Decisions\n\n| 項目 | 選択 | 理由 |\n|------|------|------|\n| 言語 | TypeScript | 既存 ingestion Worker と一貫性。MCP SDK が最も成熟 |\n| フレームワーク | Hono | ingestion と同じパターン |\n| MCP SDK | @modelcontextprotocol/sdk | プロトコル準拠保証、メンテナンスコスト低 |\n| トランスポート | Streamable HTTP | MCP 2025-03-26 仕様。Workers と相性良好 |\n| 初回スコープ | D1 のみ | 既存 raw DB で即動作確認可能 |\n\n## Architecture\n\n```\nClaude Desktop / Claude Code\n  ↓ HTTP POST (Streamable HTTP)\nCloudflare Worker (mcp-server/)\n  ├── Hono routing\n  ├── MCP SDK (McpServer)\n  │   ├── tools/d1-query\n  │   ├── tools/d1-list-tables\n  │   └── tools/d1-describe\n  └── D1 Binding (\"DB\")\n```\n\n## Directory Structure\n\n```\nmcp-server/\n├── src/\n│   ├── index.ts          # Hono entry + MCP transport\n│   ├── types.ts          # Type definitions (Env, Bindings)\n│   └── tools/\n│       └── d1.ts         # D1 tool definitions\n├── wrangler.jsonc\n├── package.json\n├── tsconfig.json\n├── biome.json\n└── vitest.config.ts\n```\n\n## Endpoints\n\n| Path | Method | Description |\n|------|--------|-------------|\n| `/health` | GET | Health check |\n| `/mcp` | POST | MCP Streamable HTTP endpoint |\n\n## Tools (Phase 1)\n\n### d1-query\n\nSQL クエリを D1 データベースに対して実行する。\n\n- **Input**: `sql` (string, required), `params` (array, optional)\n- **Output**: クエリ結果 (rows) またはステートメント結果 (changes, last_row_id)\n\n### d1-list-tables\n\nD1 データベース内のテーブル一覧を取得する。\n\n- **Input**: なし\n- **Output**: テーブル名の一覧\n\n### d1-describe\n\n指定テーブルのスキーマ情報を取得する。\n\n- **Input**: `table` (string, required)\n- **Output**: カラム名、型、制約の一覧\n\n## Authentication\n\n- `MCP_AUTH_TOKEN` を Worker Secrets で管理\n- `Authorization: Bearer <token>` ヘッダーで認証\n- 未認証リクエストは 401 で拒否\n\n## Future Phases\n\n- Phase 2: R2 (オブジェクトストレージ操作)\n- Phase 3: KV (キー・バリュー操作)\n- Phase 4: Queues (メッセージキュー操作)\n"
}

Based on the search results, here's what I found about Cloudflare Workers MCP server Streamable HTTP implementation:

## Key Implementation Details

MCP servers built with the Agents SDK use createMcpHandler to handle Streamable HTTP transport, which is the recommended approach for new MCP servers.

## Streamable HTTP Transport Benefits

The new transport simplifies how AI agents can interact with services by using a single HTTP endpoint for sending and receiving responses between the client and the server, replacing the need to implement separate endpoints for initializing the connection and for sending messages.

## Implementation Approaches

createMcpHandler() is the fastest way to get a stateless MCP server running when your tools do not need per-session state. For more complex needs, you can use createMcpHandler with a WorkerTransport instance directly in an Agent for stateful MCP servers that need to maintain session state across multiple requests, which is useful if you want to make use of advanced client features like elicitation and sampling.

## Backward Compatibility

The implementation allows your MCP server to simultaneously handle both the new Streamable HTTP transport and the existing SSE transport, maintaining backward compatibility with all remote MCP clients.

## Language Support

Cloudflare now offers first-class Python language support in Cloudflare Workers, allowing you to build MCP servers on Cloudflare that are entirely written in Python.

The documentation includes a learning resource at learnmcp.examples.workers.dev with step-by-step tutorials on building MCP servers with Cloudflare Workers.

Based on the search results, here's what I found regarding the `@modelcontextprotocol/sdk` TypeScript Streamable HTTP transport and Workers:

## Key Information:

**Streamable HTTP Transport:**
Streamable HTTP is the standard transport method for remote MCP connections, introduced in March 2025. The TypeScript SDK version 1.10.0, released on April 17, 2025, was the first to support the new Streamable HTTP transport.

**Middleware Packages:**
The SDK publishes optional "middleware" packages that help wire MCP into specific runtimes or web frameworks, including @modelcontextprotocol/node for Node.js Streamable HTTP transport wrapper.

**V2 Release Timeline:**
A stable v2 release is anticipated in Q1 2026, with v1.x remaining the recommended version for production use until then.

**Workers Support:**
MCP servers built with the Agents SDK use createMcpHandler to handle Streamable HTTP transport. Users can replace the McpAgent class with McpServer from @modelcontextprotocol/sdk and use createMcpHandler(server) in a Worker fetch handler.

The search results show that Streamable HTTP transport is now the standard for remote MCP connections and has good support across Node.js, Express, Hono, and Cloudflare Workers environments.

# createMcpHandler API Reference

## Import Path
```typescript
import { createMcpHandler, type CreateMcpHandlerOptions } from "agents/mcp";
import { McpServer } from "@modelcontextprotocol/sdk/server/mcp.js";
```

## Function Signature
```typescript
function createMcpHandler(
  server: McpServer,
  options?: CreateMcpHandlerOptions,
): (request: Request, env: Env, ctx: ExecutionContext) => Promise<Response>;
```

## Core Parameters
- **server**: An instance of `McpServer` from the MCP SDK package
- **options**: Optional configuration object for route, auth, and transport settings

## Key Configuration Options

**route** (default: `"/mcp"`)
- Specifies the URL path where the handler responds to requests

**authContext**
- Provides authentication information accessible via `getMcpAuthContext()` in tools

**transport**
- Optional custom `WorkerTransport` instance; creates a new one per request if omitted

## Stateless Server Pattern

```typescript
function createServer() {
  const server = new McpServer({
    name: "Hello MCP Server",
    version: "1.0.0",
  });

  server.tool(
    "hello",
    "Returns a greeting message",
    { name: z.string().optional() },
    async ({ name }) => {
      return {
        content: [
          {
            text: `Hello, ${name ?? "World"}!`,
            type: "text",
          },
        ],
      };
    },
  );

  return server;
}

export default {
  fetch: async (request: Request, env: Env, ctx: ExecutionContext) => {
    const server = createServer();
    return createMcpHandler(server)(request, env, ctx);
  },
} satisfies ExportedHandler<Env>;
```

## Critical Security Note

"MCP SDK 1.26.0 introduces a guard that prevents connecting to a server instance that has already been connected to a transport." Create new `McpServer` instances per request to prevent response data leakage across clients.

## Tool Definition
Tools are registered via `server.tool()` with parameters for name, description, input schema (Zod), and async handler function returning text content.

## Error Handling
The handler automatically catches errors and returns JSON-RPC error responses with code `-32603` (Internal error).
```

# Building a Stateless Remote MCP Server on Cloudflare

Based on the documentation, here's the complete guide for deploying a stateless MCP server:

## Approach Selection

The guide recommends `createMcpHandler()` for stateless implementations, described as "the fastest way to get a stateless MCP server running" when "tools do not need per-session state."

## Setup via CLI

Initialize your project using:

```bash
npm create cloudflare@latest -- remote-mcp-server-authless \
  --template=cloudflare/ai/demos/remote-mcp-authless
```

During setup, decline the AGENTS.md file, git initialization, and immediate deployment options.

## Local Development

Navigate to the project and start the development environment:

```bash
cd remote-mcp-server-authless
npm start
```

The server runs at `http://localhost:8788/mcp`.

## Testing

The guide emphasizes: "You cannot interact with the MCP server by opening the `/mcp` URL directly in a web browser. The `/mcp` endpoint expects an MCP client to send MCP protocol messages."

Instead, use the MCP Inspector in another terminal:

```bash
npx @modelcontextprotocol/inspector@latest
```

Enter `http://localhost:8788/mcp` in the inspector and select **Connect** to verify tool exposure.

## Production Deployment

Deploy via:

```bash
npx wrangler@latest deploy
```

Your server becomes accessible at `https://remote-mcp-server-authless.your-account.workers.dev/mcp`.

## Client Integration

Connect Claude Desktop or other clients using a local proxy by updating your configuration to reference the deployed URL through `mcp-remote`.

The documentation does not provide explicit TypeScript code, `wrangler.jsonc` configuration, or package dependency details for this guide—referencing the template repository instead.

```
import { McpServer } from "@modelcontextprotocol/sdk/server/mcp.js";
import { McpAgent } from "agents/mcp";
import { z } from "zod";

// Define our MCP agent with tools
export class MyMCP extends McpAgent {
	server = new McpServer({
		name: "Authless Calculator",
		version: "1.0.0",
	});

	async init() {
		// Simple addition tool
		this.server.tool("add", { a: z.number(), b: z.number() }, async ({ a, b }) => ({
			content: [{ type: "text", text: String(a + b) }],
		}));

		// Calculator tool with multiple operations
		this.server.tool(
			"calculate",
			{
				operation: z.enum(["add", "subtract", "multiply", "divide"]),
				a: z.number(),
				b: z.number(),
			},
			async ({ operation, a, b }) => {
				let result: number;
				switch (operation) {
					case "add":
						result = a + b;
						break;
					case "subtract":
						result = a - b;
						break;
					case "multiply":
						result = a * b;
						break;
					case "divide":
						if (b === 0)
							return {
								content: [
									{
										type: "text",
										text: "Error: Cannot divide by zero",
									},
								],
							};
						result = a / b;
						break;
				}
				return { content: [{ type: "text", text: String(result) }] };
			},
		);
	}
}

export default {
	fetch(request: Request, env: Env, ctx: ExecutionContext) {
		const url = new URL(request.url);

		if (url.pathname === "/mcp") {
			return MyMCP.serve("/mcp").fetch(request, env, ctx);
		}

		return new Response("Not found", { status: 404 });
	},
};
```

> AGENT

`★ Insight ─────────────────────────────────────`
Cloudflareの`agents`パッケージが`createMcpHandler`を提供し、`McpServer`と組み合わせることでDurable ObjectsなしのステートレスなリモートMCPサーバーが実現できます。セキュリティ上、**リクエストごとにMcpServerインスタンスを新規作成**する必要があります（SDK 1.26.0+の制約）。
`─────────────────────────────────────────────────`

正確なバージョンを揃えるため、ingestionの設定を確認します。

```
     1→# MCP Cloudflare Server - Phase 1: D1 Design
     2→
     3→## Overview
     4→
     5→Cloudflare D1 を操作できる MCP サーバーを TypeScript/Hono で実装する。
     6→Claude Desktop や Claude Code から自然言語で D1 データベースを操作できるようになる。
     7→
     8→Linear Issue: [TA-216](https://linear.app/ta93abe/issue/TA-216)
     9→
    10→## Decisions
    11→
    12→| 項目 | 選択 | 理由 |
    13→|------|------|------|
    14→| 言語 | TypeScript | 既存 ingestion Worker と一貫性。MCP SDK が最も成熟 |
    15→| フレームワーク | Hono | ingestion と同じパターン |
    16→| MCP SDK | @modelcontextprotocol/sdk | プロトコル準拠保証、メンテナンスコスト低 |
    17→| トランスポート | Streamable HTTP | MCP 2025-03-26 仕様。Workers と相性良好 |
    18→| 初回スコープ | D1 のみ | 既存 raw DB で即動作確認可能 |
    19→
    20→## Architecture
    21→
    22→```
    23→Claude Desktop / Claude Code
    24→  ↓ HTTP POST (Streamable HTTP)
    25→Cloudflare Worker (mcp-server/)
    26→  ├── Hono routing
    27→  ├── MCP SDK (McpServer)
    28→  │   ├── tools/d1-query
    29→  │   ├── tools/d1-list-tables
    30→  │   └── tools/d1-describe
    31→  └── D1 Binding ("DB")
    32→```
    33→
    34→## Directory Structure
    35→
    36→```
    37→mcp-server/
    38→├── src/
    39→│   ├── index.ts          # Hono entry + MCP transport
    40→│   ├── types.ts          # Type definitions (Env, Bindings)
    41→│   └── tools/
    42→│       └── d1.ts         # D1 tool definitions
    43→├── wrangler.jsonc
    44→├── package.json
    45→├── tsconfig.json
    46→├── biome.json
    47→└── vitest.config.ts
    48→```
    49→
    50→## Endpoints
    51→
    52→| Path | Method | Description |
    53→|------|--------|-------------|
    54→| `/health` | GET | Health check |
    55→| `/mcp` | POST | MCP Streamable HTTP endpoint |
    56→
    57→## Tools (Phase 1)
    58→
    59→### d1-query
    60→
    61→SQL クエリを D1 データベースに対して実行する。
    62→
    63→- **Input**: `sql` (string, required), `params` (array, optional)
    64→- **Output**: クエリ結果 (rows) またはステートメント結果 (changes, last_row_id)
    65→
    66→### d1-list-tables
    67→
    68→D1 データベース内のテーブル一覧を取得する。
    69→
    70→- **Input**: なし
    71→- **Output**: テーブル名の一覧
    72→
    73→### d1-describe
    74→
    75→指定テーブルのスキーマ情報を取得する。
    76→
    77→- **Input**: `table` (string, required)
    78→- **Output**: カラム名、型、制約の一覧
    79→
    80→## Authentication
    81→
    82→- `MCP_AUTH_TOKEN` を Worker Secrets で管理
    83→- `Authorization: Bearer <token>` ヘッダーで認証
    84→- 未認証リクエストは 401 で拒否
    85→
    86→## Future Phases
    87→
    88→- Phase 2: R2 (オブジェクトストレージ操作)
    89→- Phase 3: KV (キー・バリュー操作)
    90→- Phase 4: Queues (メッセージキュー操作)
    91→
```

  "content": "# MCP Cloudflare D1 Server - Implementation Plan\n\n> **For Claude:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task.\n\n**Goal:** Cloudflare D1 を操作できる MCP サーバーを TypeScript/Hono で実装し、Claude Desktop/Code から自然言語でデータベース操作を可能にする。\n\n**Architecture:** Cloudflare Workers 上に Hono + MCP SDK (`@modelcontextprotocol/sdk`) でステートレスな MCP サーバーを構築。`agents` パッケージの `createMcpHandler` で Streamable HTTP トランスポートを提供し、D1 バインディング経由でデータベースを操作する。リクエストごとに McpServer インスタンスを新規作成（SDK 1.26.0+ のセキュリティ要件）。\n\n**Tech Stack:** TypeScript, Hono, @modelcontextprotocol/sdk, agents (createMcpHandler), Zod 4, Vitest + @cloudflare/vitest-pool-workers\n\n**Linear Issue:** [TA-216](https://linear.app/ta93abe/issue/TA-216)\n\n**Branch:** `feat/mcp-cloudflare-d1`\n\n---\n\n### Task 1: Project Scaffolding\n\n**Files:**\n- Create: `mcp-server/package.json`\n- Create: `mcp-server/wrangler.jsonc`\n- Create: `mcp-server/tsconfig.json`\n- Create: `mcp-server/biome.json`\n- Create: `mcp-server/vitest.config.ts`\n- Remove: `mcp-server/.gitkeep`\n\n**Step 1: Create package.json**\n\n```json\n{\n  \"name\": \"mcp-cloudflare\",\n  \"type\": \"module\",\n  \"version\": \"0.0.0\",\n  \"private\": true,\n  \"scripts\": {\n    \"dev\": \"wrangler dev\",\n    \"deploy\": \"wrangler deploy --minify\",\n    \"test\": \"vitest\",\n    \"test:run\": \"vitest run\",\n    \"typecheck\": \"tsc --noEmit\",\n    \"cf-typegen\": \"wrangler types --env-interface CloudflareBindings\",\n    \"lint\": \"biome lint .\",\n    \"lint:fix\": \"biome lint --write .\",\n    \"format\": \"biome format --write .\",\n    \"check\": \"biome check .\",\n    \"check:fix\": \"biome check --write .\"\n  },\n  \"dependencies\": {\n    \"agents\": \"^0.5.0\",\n    \"hono\": \"^4.11.7\",\n    \"zod\": \"^4.3.6\"\n  },\n  \"devDependencies\": {\n    \"@biomejs/biome\": \"^2.3.13\",\n    \"@cloudflare/vitest-pool-workers\": \"^0.8.0\",\n    \"@cloudflare/workers-types\": \"^4.20250124.0\",\n    \"typescript\": \"^5.7.0\",\n    \"vitest\": \"^3.0.0\",\n    \"wrangler\": \"^4.67.0\"\n  }\n}\n```\n\n**Step 2: Create wrangler.jsonc**\n\n```jsonc\n{\n  \"$schema\": \"node_modules/wrangler/config-schema.json\",\n  \"name\": \"mcp-cloudflare\",\n  \"main\": \"src/index.ts\",\n  \"compatibility_date\": \"2026-02-27\",\n  \"compatibility_flags\": [\"nodejs_compat\"],\n  \"observability\": {\n    \"enabled\": true,\n    \"head_sampling_rate\": 1\n  },\n  \"d1_databases\": [\n    {\n      \"binding\": \"DB\",\n      \"database_name\": \"raw\",\n      \"database_id\": \"30fb49b7-8321-4792-8706-06c9205ce026\"\n    }\n  ]\n  // シークレット:\n  //   wrangler secret put MCP_AUTH_TOKEN\n}\n```\n\n**Step 3: Create tsconfig.json** (ingestion と同じ、jsx 不要)\n\n```json\n{\n  \"compilerOptions\": {\n    \"target\": \"ESNext\",\n    \"module\": \"ESNext\",\n    \"moduleResolution\": \"Bundler\",\n    \"strict\": true,\n    \"skipLibCheck\": true,\n    \"lib\": [\"ESNext\"],\n    \"types\": [\"@cloudflare/workers-types/experimental\", \"@cloudflare/vitest-pool-workers\"]\n  },\n  \"include\": [\"src/**/*.ts\"],\n  \"exclude\": [\"node_modules\"]\n}\n```\n\n**Step 4: Create biome.json** (ingestion と同一)\n\n**Step 5: Create vitest.config.ts** (ingestion と同一)\n\n**Step 6: Install dependencies**\n\nRun: `cd mcp-server && pnpm install`\n\n**Step 7: Remove .gitkeep and commit**\n\n```bash\nrm mcp-server/.gitkeep\ngit add mcp-server/\ngit commit -m \"chore: scaffold mcp-server project [TA-216]\"\n```\n\n---\n\n### Task 2: Types + Hono Health Endpoint\n\n**Files:**\n- Create: `mcp-server/src/types.ts`\n- Create: `mcp-server/src/index.ts`\n- Create: `mcp-server/src/__tests__/index.test.ts`\n\n**Step 1: Write the failing test**\n\n```typescript\n// src/__tests__/index.test.ts\nimport { describe, expect, it } from \"vitest\";\nimport { SELF } from \"cloudflare:test\";\n\ndescribe(\"MCP Server\", () => {\n  it(\"should return OK on health check\", async () => {\n    const response = await SELF.fetch(\"http://localhost/health\");\n    expect(response.status).toBe(200);\n    expect(await response.text()).toBe(\"OK\");\n  });\n\n  it(\"should return 404 on unknown routes\", async () => {\n    const response = await SELF.fetch(\"http://localhost/unknown\");\n    expect(response.status).toBe(404);\n  });\n});\n```\n\n**Step 2: Run test to verify it fails**\n\nRun: `cd mcp-server && pnpm test:run`\nExpected: FAIL (no src/index.ts)\n\n**Step 3: Write types and implementation**\n\n```typescript\n// src/types.ts\nexport type Bindings = {\n  DB: D1Database;\n  MCP_AUTH_TOKEN: string;\n};\n```\n\n```typescript\n// src/index.ts\nimport { Hono } from \"hono\";\nimport type { Bindings } from \"./types\";\n\nconst app = new Hono<{ Bindings: Bindings }>();\n\napp.get(\"/health\", (c) => c.text(\"OK\"));\n\nexport default app;\n```\n\n**Step 4: Run test to verify it passes**\n\nRun: `cd mcp-server && pnpm test:run`\nExpected: PASS\n\n**Step 5: Commit**\n\n```bash\ngit add mcp-server/src/\ngit commit -m \"feat: add types and health endpoint for mcp-server [TA-216]\"\n```\n\n---\n\n### Task 3: D1 list-tables Tool\n\n**Files:**\n- Create: `mcp-server/src/tools/d1.ts`\n- Create: `mcp-server/src/__tests__/tools/d1.test.ts`\n\n**Step 1: Write the failing test**\n\n```typescript\n// src/__tests__/tools/d1.test.ts\nimport { describe, expect, it, beforeEach } from \"vitest\";\nimport { env } from \"cloudflare:test\";\nimport { listTables } from \"../../tools/d1\";\n\ndescribe(\"d1-list-tables\", () => {\n  beforeEach(async () => {\n    await env.DB.exec(\"CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS test_users (id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, name TEXT)\");\n    await env.DB.exec(\"CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS test_events (id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, type TEXT)\");\n  });\n\n  it(\"should return table names\", async () => {\n    const result = await await queryD1(\n      env.DB,\n      \"INSERT INTO test_users (id, name) VALUES (?, ?)\",\n      [3, \"Charlie\"]\n    );\n    expect(result.meta.changes).toBe(1);\n  });\n});\n```\n\n**Step 2: Run params?: unknown[]\n): Promise<QueryResult> {\n  const stmt = db.prepare(sql);\n  const bound = params && params.length > 0 ? stmt.bind(...params) : stmt;\n  const result = await bound.all();\n  return {\n    results: result.results as Record<string, unknown>[],\n    meta: {\n      changes: result.meta?.changes ?? 0,\n      last_row_id: result.meta?.last_row_id ?? 0,\n      duration: \"@modelcontextprotocol/sdk/server/mcp.js\";\nimport { z } from \"zod\";\nimport type { Bindings } from \"./types\";\nimport { listTables, describeTable, queryD1 } from \"./tools/d1\";\n\nexport function createMcpServer(env: Bindings): McpServer {\n  const server = new McpServer({\n    name: \"mcp-cloudflare\",\n    version: \"1.0.0\",\n  });\n\n  server.tool(\"d1-list-tables\", \"List all tables in the D1 database\", async () => {\n    const tables = await listTables(env.DB);\n    return {\n      content: [{ type: \"text\", text: JSON.stringify(tables, null, 2) }],\n    };\n  });\n\n  server.tool(\n    \"d1-describe\",\n    \"Get schema information for a D1 table\",\n    { table: z.string().describe(\"Table name to describe\") },\n    async ({ table }) => {\n      const columns = await describeTable(env.DB, table);\n      return {\n        content: [{ type: \"text\", text: JSON.stringify(columns, null, 2) }],\n      };\n    }\n  );\n\n  server.tool(\n    \"d1-query\",\n    \"Execute a SQL query against the D1 database\",\n    {\n      sql: z.string().describe(\"SQL query to execute\"),\n      params: z.array(z.unknown()).optional().describe(\"Query parameters for prepared statement\"),\n    },\n    async ({ sql, params }) => {\n      const result = await queryD1(env.DB, sql, params);\n      return {\n        content: [{ type: \"text\", text: JSON.stringify(result, null, 2) }],\n      };\n    }\n  );\n\n  return server;\n}\n```\n\n**Step 2: Wire MCP handler into index.ts**\n\n```typescript\n// src/index.ts\nimport { Hono } from }>();\n\napp.get(\"/health\", (c) => c.text(\"OK\"));\n\napp.all(\"/mcp\", async (c) => {\n  const server = createMcpServer(c.env);\n  const handler = createMcpHandler(server);\n  return handler(c.req.raw, c.env, c.executionCtx);\n});\n\nexport default app;\n```\n\n**Step 3: Run existing tests to verify nothing breaks**\n\nRun: `cd mcp-server && pnpm test:run`\nExpected: All src/__tests__/middleware/auth.test.ts\nimport { describe, expect, it } from \"vitest\";\nimport { SELF } from \"cloudflare:test\";\n\ndescribe(\"Auth Middleware\", () => {\n  it(\"should allow health check without auth\", async () => {\n    const response = await SELF.fetch(\"http://localhost/health\");\n    expect(response.status).toBe(200);\n  });\n\n  it(\"should reject /mcp without auth header\", async () => {\n    const response = await SELF.fetch(\"http://localhost/mcp\", { method: \"POST\" });\n    expect(response.status).toBe(401);\n  });\n\n  it(\"should reject /mcp with invalid expect(response.status).toBe(401);\n  });\n});\n```\n\n**Step 2: Run test to verify it fails**\n\nRun: `cd mcp-server && pnpm test:run`\nExpected: FAIL (no auth check yet)\n\n**Step 3: Write implementation**\n\n```typescript\n// src/middleware/auth.ts\nimport type { Context, Next } from \"hono\";\nimport type { Bindings } from \"../types\";\n\nexport async function bearerAuth(c: Context<{ Bindings: Bindings }>, next: Next) {\n  // Skip auth for health check\n  if (c.req.path === \"/health\") {\n    return next();\n  }\n\n  const authHeader = c.req.header(\"Authorization\");\n  if (!authHeader?.startsWith(\"Bearer \")) {\n    return c.json({ error: \"Missing or invalid Authorization header\" }, 401);\n  }\n\n  const token = authHeader.slice(7);\n  if (token !== c.env.MCP_AUTH_TOKEN) {\n    return c.json({ error: \"Invalid token\" }, 401);\n  }\n\n  return next();\n}\n```\n\nUpdate index.ts to use middleware:\n\n```typescript\n// src/index.ts - add \"../../tools/d1\";\n\ndescribe(\"d1-query\", () => {\n  beforeEach(async () => {\n    await env.DB.exec(\"CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS test_users (id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, name TEXT)\");\n    await env.DB.exec(\"DELETE FROM test_users\");\n    await env.DB.exec(\"INSERT INTO test_users (id, name) VALUES (1, 'Alice'), (2, 'Bob')\");\n  });\n\n  it(\"should execute SELECT query\", async () => {\n    const result = await queryD1(env.DB, \"SELECT * FROM test_users ORDER BY id\");\n    expect(result.results).toHaveLength(2);\n    expect(result.results[0]).toEqual({ id: 1, name: \"Alice\" });\n  });\n\n  it(\"should execute parameterized query\", async () => {\n    const result = await queryD1(env.DB, \"SELECT * FROM test_users WHERE name = ?\", [\"Bob\"]);\n    expect(result.results).toHaveLength(1);\n    expect(result.results[0]).toEqual({ id: 2, name: \"Bob\" });\n  });\n\n  it(\"should execute INSERT and return changes\", async () => {\n    const result = await queryD1(\n      env.DB,\n      \"INSERT INTO test_users (id, name) VALUES (?, ?)\",\n      [3, \"Charlie\"]\n    );\n    expect(result.meta.changes).toBe(1);\n  });\n});\n```\n\n**Step 2: Run test to verify it fails**\n\nRun: `cd mcp-server && pnpm test:run`\nExpected: FAIL\n\n**Step 3: Write implementation**\n\n```typescript\n// Add to src/tools/d1.ts\nexport interface QueryResult {\n  results: Record<string, unknown>[];\n  meta: {\n    changes: number;\n    last_row_id: number;\n    duration: number;\n  };\n}\n\nexport async function queryD1(\n  db: D1Database,\n  sql: string,\n  params?: unknown[]\n): Promise<QueryResult> {\n  const stmt = db.prepare(sql);\n  const bound = params && params.length > 0 ? stmt.bind(...params) : stmt;\n  const result = await bound.all();\n  return {\n    results: result.results as Record<string, unknown>[],\n    meta: {\n      changes: result.meta?.changes ?? 0,\n      last_row_id: result.meta?.last_row_id ?? 0,\n      duration: result.meta?.duration ?? 0,\n    },\n  };\n}\n```\n\n**Step 4: Run test to verify it passes**\n\nRun: `cd mcp-server && pnpm test:run`\nExpected: PASS\n\n**Step 5: Commit**\n\n```\nfeat: add d1-query tool [TA-216]\n```\n\n---\n\n### Task 6: MCP Server Integration\n\n**Files:**\n- Create: `mcp-server/src/server.ts`\n- Modify: `mcp-server/src/index.ts`\n\n**Step 1: Create MCP server factory**\n\n```typescript\n// src/server.ts\nimport { McpServer } from \"@modelcontextprotocol/sdk/server/mcp.js\";\nimport { z } from \"zod\";\nimport type { Bindings } from \"./types\";\nimport { listTables, describeTable, queryD1 } from \"./tools/d1\";\n\nexport function createMcpServer(env: Bindings): McpServer {\n  const server = new McpServer({\n    name: \"mcp-cloudflare\",\n    version: \"1.0.0\",\n  });\n\n  server.tool(\"d1-list-tables\", \"List all tables in the D1 database\", async () => ({\n    content: [{ type: \"text\", text: JSON.stringify(await listTables(env.DB), null, 2) }],\n  }));\n\n  server.tool(\n    \"d1-describe\",\n    \"Get schema information for a D1 table\",\n    { table: z.string().describe(\"Table name to describe\") },\n    async ({ table }) => ({\n      content: [{ type: \"text\", text: JSON.stringify(await describeTable(env.DB, table), null, 2) }],\n    })\n  );\n\n  server.tool(\n    \"d1-query\",\n    \"Execute a SQL query against the D1 database\",\n    {\n      sql: z.string().describe(\"SQL query to execute\"),\n      params: z.array(z.unknown()).optional().describe(\"Query parameters for prepared statement\"),\n    },\n    async ({ sql, params }) => ({\n      content: [{ type: \"text\", text: JSON.stringify(await queryD1(env.DB, sql, params), null, 2) }],\n    })\n  );\n\n  return server;\n}\n```\n\n**Step 2: Wire MCP handler into index.ts**\n\n```typescript\n// src/index.ts\nimport { Hono } from \"hono\";\nimport { createMcpHandler } from \"agents/mcp\";\nimport type { Bindings } from \"./types\";\nimport { createMcpServer } from \"./server\";\n\nconst app = new Hono<{ Bindings: Bindings }>();\n\napp.get(\"/health\", (c) => c.text(\"OK\"));\n\napp.all(\"/mcp\", async (c) => {\n  const server = createMcpServer(c.env);\n  const handler = createMcpHandler(server);\n  return handler(c.req.raw, c.env, c.executionCtx);\n});\n\nexport default app;\n```\n\n**Step 3: Run existing tests to verify nothing breaks**\n\nRun: `cd mcp-server && pnpm test:run`\nExpected: All PASS\n\n**Step 4: Manual test with MCP Inspector**\n\nRun: `cd mcp-server && pnpm dev` (terminal 1)\nRun: `npx @modelcontextprotocol/inspector@latest` (terminal 2)\n- Connect to `http://localhost:8787/mcp`\n- Verify 3 tools appear: d1-list-tables, d1-describe, d1-query\n- Test d1-list-tables\n\n**Step 5: Commit**\n\n```\nfeat: integrate D1 tools with MCP server [TA-216]\n```\n\n---\n\n### Task 7: Bearer Token Auth Middleware\n\n**Files:**\n- Create: `mcp-server/src/middleware/auth.ts`\n- Create: `mcp-server/src/__tests__/middleware/auth.test.ts`\n- Modify: `mcp-server/src/index.ts`\n\n**Step 1: Write the failing test**\n\n```typescript\n// src/__tests__/middleware/auth.test.ts\nimport { describe, expect, it } from \"vitest\";\nimport { SELF } from \"cloudflare:test\";\n\ndescribe(\"Auth Middleware\", () => {\n  it(\"should allow health check without auth\", async () => {\n    const response = await SELF.fetch(\"http://localhost/health\");\n    expect(response.status).toBe(200);\n  });\n\n  it(\"should reject /mcp without auth header\", async () => {\n    const response = await SELF.fetch(\"http://localhost/mcp\", { method: \"POST\" });\n    expect(response.status).toBe(401);\n  });\n\n  it(\"should reject /mcp with invalid token\", async () => {\n    const response = await SELF.fetch(\"http://localhost/mcp\", {\n      method: \"POST\",\n      headers: { Authorization: \"Bearer wrong-token\" },\n    });\n    expect(response.status).toBe(401);\n  });\n});\n```\n\n**Step 2: Run test to verify it fails**\n\nRun: `cd mcp-server && pnpm test:run`\nExpected: FAIL (no auth check)\n\n**Step 3: Write implementation**\n\n```typescript\n// src/middleware/auth.ts\nimport type { Context, Next } from \"hono\";\nimport type { Bindings } from \"../types\";\n\nexport async function bearerAuth(c: Context<{ Bindings: Bindings }>, next: Next) {\n  if (c.req.path === \"/health\") {\n    return next();\n  }\n\n  const authHeader = c.req.header(\"Authorization\");\n  if (!authHeader?.startsWith(\"Bearer \")) {\n    return c.json({ error: \"Missing or invalid Authorization header\" }, 401);\n  }\n\n  const token = authHeader.slice(7);\n  if (token !== c.env.MCP_AUTH_TOKEN) {\n    return c.json({ error: \"Invalid token\" }, 401);\n  }\n\n  return next();\n}\n```\n\nUpdate index.ts:\n```typescript\nimport { bearerAuth } from \"./middleware/auth\";\napp.use(\"*\", bearerAuth);\n```\n\n**Step 4: Run test to verify it passes**\n\nRun: `cd mcp-server && pnpm test:run`\nExpected: All PASS\n\n**Step 5: Commit**\n\n```\nfeat: add bearer token auth middleware [TA-216]\n```\n\n---\n\n## References\n\n- [MCP TypeScript Server](https://developers.cloudflare.com/agents/guides/remote-mcp-server/)\n- [Streamable HTTP Transport](https://blog.cloudflare.com/streamable-http-mcp-servers-python/)\n- [MCP Transports ## Task 1: Project Scaffolding

`★ Insight ─────────────────────────────────────`
`agents` パッケージは Cloudflare の MCP ランタイムで、`createMcpHandler` を提供します。`nodejs_compat` フラグが必要なのは、MCP SDK が内部で Node.js の `EventEmitter` 等を使用するためです。ingestion Worker にはこのフラグがないのはここが新しいポイント。
`─────────────────────────────────────────────────`

## Task 3: D1 list-tables Tool

`★ Insight ─────────────────────────────────────`
D1 は SQLite ベースなので、`sqlite_master` テーブルからメタデータを取得します。`_cf_` プレフィックスは Cloudflare 内部テーブルなのでフィルタ対象。`@cloudflare/vitest-pool-workers` のおかげで、テスト内でリアルな D1 が使えるのがポイントです。
`─────────────────────────────────────────────────`

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/ta93abe/Developer/github.com/ta93abe/data-engineering-with-cloudflare/mcp-server/src/tools/d1.ts",
  "old_string": "  return result.results.map((row) => row.name);\n}",
  "new_string": "  return result.results.map((row) => row.name);\n}\n\nexport interface ColumnInfo {\n  cid: number;\n  name: string;\n  type: string;\n  notnull: number;\n  dflt_value: string | null;\n  pk: number;\n}\n\nexport async function describeTable(db: D1Database, table: string): Promise<ColumnInfo[]> {\n  if (!/^[a-zA-Z_][a-zA-Z0-9_]*$/.test(table)) {\n    throw new Error(`Invalid table name: ${table}`);\n  }\n  const result = await db.prepare(`PRAGMA table_info(${table})`).all<ColumnInfo>();\n  if (result.results.length === 0) {\n    throw new Error(`Table not found: ${table}`);\n  }\n  return result.results;\n}"
}

## Task 5: D1 query Tool

`★ Insight ─────────────────────────────────────`
`queryD1` はユーザーが任意の SQL を実行できるツールです。パラメータ化クエリ（`?` プレースホルダ）をサポートすることで、MCP クライアント側での SQL インジェクション対策を容易にしています。D1 の `stmt.bind()` でパラメータバインドします。
`─────────────────────────────────────────────────`

}

{
  "replace_all": false,

  "old_string": "  return result.results;\n}",
  "new_string": "  return result.results;\n}\n\nexport interface QueryResult {\n  results: Record<string, unknown>[];\n  meta: {\n    changes: number;\n    last_row_id: number;\n    duration: number;\n  };\n}\n\nexport async function queryD1(\n  db: D1Database,\n  sql: string,\n  params?: unknown[]\n): Promise<QueryResult> {\n  const stmt = db.prepare(sql);\n  const bound = params && params.length > 0 ? stmt.bind(...params) : stmt;\n  const result = await bound.all();\n  return {\n    results: result.results as Record<string, unknown>[],\n    meta: {\n      changes: result.meta?.changes ?? 0,\n      last_row_id: result.meta?.last_row_id ?? 0,\n      duration: result.meta?.duration ?? 0,\n    },\n  };\n}"
}

## Task 6: MCP Server Integration

ここが核心部分です。D1ツールを MCP SDK に登録し、`createMcpHandler` で Streamable HTTP を提供します。

`★ Insight ─────────────────────────────────────`
MCP SDK 1.26.0+ では、セキュリティ上の理由からリクエストごとに `McpServer` を新規生成する必要があります。`createMcpServer(env)` ファクトリパターンで、環境変数（D1バインディング）をクロージャ経由でツールハンドラに渡します。これにより、レスポンスデータがリクエスト間でリークしません。
`─────────────────────────────────────────────────`

ここは設計判断が重要な部分です。`createMcpServer` ファクトリのコードを書いてもらいたいのですが、まず私がファイルと構造を準備します。

> TOOL

tool_use Write
id: toolu_011e92yigN61z9uVqeGqBJCJ
```json
{
  "file_path": "/Users/ta93abe/Developer/github.com/ta93abe/data-engineering-with-cloudflare/mcp-server/src/server.ts",
  "content": "import { McpServer } from \"@modelcontextprotocol/sdk/server/mcp.js\";\nimport { z } from \"zod\";\nimport type { Bindings } from \"./types\";\nimport { describeTable, listTables, queryD1 } from \"./tools/d1\";\n\nexport function createMcpServer(env: Bindings): McpServer {\n  const server = new McpServer({\n    name: \"mcp-cloudflare\",\n    version: \"1.0.0\",\n  });\n\n  server.tool(\"d1-list-tables\", \"List all tables in the D1 database\", async () => ({\n    content: [{ type: \"text\", text: JSON.stringify(await listTables(env.DB), null, 2) }],\n  }));\n\n  server.tool(\n    \"d1-describe\",\n    \"Get schema information for a D1 table\",\n    { table: z.string().describe(\"Table name to describe\") },\n    async ({ table }) => ({\n      content: [\n        { type: \"text\", text: JSON.stringify(await describeTable(env.DB, table), null, 2) },\n      ],\n    })\n  );\n\n  server.tool(\n    \"d1-query\",\n    \"Execute a SQL query against the D1 database\",\n    {\n      sql: z.string().describe(\"SQL query to execute\"),\n      params: z.array(z.unknown()).optional().describe(\"Query parameters for prepared statement\"),\n    },\n    async ({ sql, params }) => ({\n      content: [{ type: \"text\", text: JSON.stringify(await queryD1(env.DB, sql, params), null, 2) }],\n    })\n  );\n\n  return server;\n}\n"
}
```

{
  "file_path": "/Users/ta93abe/Developer/github.com/ta93abe/data-engineering-with-cloudflare/mcp-server/src/middleware/auth.ts",
  "content": "import type { Context, Next } from \"hono\";\nimport type { Bindings } from \"../types\";\n\nexport async function bearerAuth(c: Context<{ Bindings: Bindings }>, next: Next) {\n  if (c.req.path === \"/health\") {\n    return next();\n  }\n\n  const authHeader = c.req.header(\"Authorization\");\n  if (!authHeader?.startsWith(\"Bearer \")) {\n    return c.json({ error: \"Missing or invalid Authorization header\" }, 401);\n  }\n\n  const token = authHeader.slice(7);\n  if (token !== c.env.MCP_AUTH_TOKEN) {\n    return c.json({ error: \"Invalid token\" }, 401);\n  }\n\n  return next();\n}\n"
}
```

{
  "command": "gh pr create --title \"feat: Implement MCP server for Cloudflare D1 [TA-216]\" --body \"$(cat <<'EOF'\n## Summary\n- Cloudflare D1 を操作できる MCP サーバーを TypeScript/Hono で実装\n- MCP SDK (`@modelcontextprotocol/sdk`) + `agents` パッケージの `createMcpHandler` で Streamable HTTP トランスポートを提供\n- 3つの D1 ツール: `d1-list-tables`, `d1-describe`, `d1-query`\n- Bearer token 認証ミドルウェア（`/health` はバイパス）\n- 15テスト全合格（Vitest + @cloudflare/vitest-pool-workers）\n\n### Tools\n| Tool | Description |\n|------|-------------|\n| `d1-list-tables` | D1内のテーブル一覧取得 |\n| `d1-describe` | テーブルスキーマ（カラム情報）取得 |\n| `d1-query` | SQLクエリ実行（パラメータ化対応） |\n\n### Architecture\n```\nClaude Desktop / Claude Code\n  ↓ HTTP POST (Streamable HTTP)\nCloudflare Worker (mcp-server/)\n  ├── Hono routing + Bearer auth\n  ├── MCP SDK (McpServer per request)\n  └── D1 Binding (\"DB\")\n```\n\n## Test plan\n- [x] `pnpm test:run` - 15テスト全合格\n- [ ] `pnpm dev` + MCP Inspector でローカル動作確認\n- [ ] `wrangler secret put MCP_AUTH_TOKEN` でシークレット設定\n- [ ] デプロイ後、Claude Desktop から接続確認\n\nCloses TA-216\n\n🤖 Generated with [Claude Code](https://claude.com/claude-code)\nEOF\n)\" --label \"minor\"",

`token !== c.env.MCP_AUTH_TOKEN` での文字列比較は、タイミング攻撃に対して脆弱な可能性があります。攻撃者は応答時間の差異を分析することで、トークンを1文字ずつ推測できてしまうかもしれません。

セキュリティトークンの比較には、定数時間で比較を行う関数を使用することが推奨されます。Honoは `hono/utils/crypto` の `timingSafeEqual` ユーティリティを提供しています。

この修正を適用するには、まずファイルの先頭で `import { timingSafeEqual } from "hono/utils/crypto";` を追加してください。

```suggestion
  const token = authHeader.slice(7);
  const expected = c.env.MCP_AUTH_TOKEN;
  if (token.length !== expected.length || !timingSafeEqual(token, expected)) {
    return c.json({ error: "Invalid token" }, 401);
  }
```

[coderabbitai[bot]] mcp-server/src/tools/d1.ts:4
_⚠️ Potential issue_ | _🟡 Minor_

<details>
<summary>🧩 Analysis chain</summary>

🌐 Web query:

`SQLite LIKE wildcard underscore behavior single character escape`

💡 Result:

In SQLite, the underscore (`_`) in a `LIKE` pattern is a **single-character wildcard** (matches exactly one character). The percent sign (`%`) matches **zero or more characters**. [1]

To match a **literal underscore** (not a wildcard), use the optional **`ESCAPE`** clause, which defines a **single-character** escape prefix:

```sql
-- match any string containing a literal underscore:
WHERE col LIKE '%\_%' ESCAPE '\';
```

With `ESCAPE '\'`, the sequences `\_`, `\%`, and `\\` in the pattern match a literal `_`, literal `%`, and literal `\`, respectively. [1]

---

🌐 Web query:

`SQLite GLOB vs LIKE difference wildcards patterns`

💡 Result:

In SQLite, `LIKE` and `GLOB` both do pattern matching, but they use different wildcard syntaxes and different case-sensitivity rules.

### Wildcards / pattern syntax
**`LIKE` (SQL-standard wildcards)** ([sqlite.org](https://sqlite.org/lang_expr.html?utm_source=openai))  
- `%` = any sequence of 0+ characters  
- `_` = exactly 1 character  
- Supports an **`ESCAPE`** clause to treat `%` / `_` (or the escape char itself) literally. ([sqlite.org](https://sqlite.org/lang_expr.html?utm_source=openai))  

**`GLOB` (Unix-file-glob wildcards)** ([sqlite.org](https://sqlite.org/lang_expr.html?utm_source=openai))  
- `*` = any sequence of 0+ characters  
- `?` = exactly 1 character  
- Also supports bracket patterns like `[abc]`, `[a-z]`, `[^abc]` (character sets/ranges). ([sqlitetutorial.net](https://www.sqlitetutorial.net/sqlite-glob/?utm_source=openai))  
- No `ESCAPE` clause (so “escaping” is typically done via bracket forms like `[*]` for a literal `*`).

### Case sensitivity
- `LIKE` is **case-insensitive for ASCII A–Z by default** (but Unicode beyond ASCII is case-sensitive by default). ([sqlite.org](https://sqlite.org/lang_expr.html?utm_source=openai))  
- `LIKE` can be made case-sensitive via `PRAGMA case_sensitive_like=ON;`. ([sqlite.org](https://sqlite.org/lang_expr.html?utm_source=openai))  
- `GLOB` is **case-sensitive**. ([sqlite.org](https://sqlite.org/lang_expr.html?utm_source=openai))  

### Quick examples
- `name LIKE 'Abc%'` matches `Abcdef` (and also `abcdef` by default for ASCII). ([sqlite.org](https://sqlite.org/lang_expr.html?utm_source=openai))  
- `name GLOB 'Abc*'` matches `Abcdef` but **not** `abcdef`. ([sqlite.org](https://sqlite.org/lang_expr.html?utm_source=openai))  

### Index usage (performance note)
SQLite’s optimizer can use indexes for some `LIKE`/`GLOB` patterns, but the required index collation differs (e.g., `GLOB` needs `BINARY`; `LIKE` needs `NOCASE` vs `BINARY` depending on `case_sensitive_like`). ([sqlite.org](https://sqlite.org/optoverview.html?utm_source=openai))

Citations:

- 1: https://sqlite.org/lang_expr.html?utm_source=openai
- 2: https://sqlite.org/lang_expr.html?utm_source=openai
- 3: https://sqlite.org/lang_expr.html?utm_source=openai
- 4: https://www.sqlitetutorial.net/sqlite-glob/?utm_source=openai
- 5: https://sqlite.org/lang_expr.html?utm_source=openai
- 6: https://sqlite.org/lang_expr.html?utm_source=openai
- 7: https://sqlite.org/lang_expr.html?utm_source=openai
- 8: https://sqlite.org/lang_expr.html?utm_source=openai
- 9: https://sqlite.org/lang_expr.html?utm_source=openai
- 10: https://sqlite.org/optoverview.html?utm_source=openai

---



</details>

**`_cf_` filter is too broad due to LIKE wildcard behavior.**

The `NOT LIKE '_cf_%'` pattern treats `_` as a single-character wildcard in SQLite, unintentionally excluding valid user tables like `acf_data`. The underscore in the second position causes it to match `[any char]cf[anything]`, rather than only tables starting with the literal `_cf_` prefix that Cloudflare generates.

Use `GLOB` instead, where `_` is treated as a literal underscore:

<details>
<summary>✅ Suggested fix</summary>

```diff
-      "SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table' AND name NOT LIKE 'sqlite_%' AND name NOT LIKE '_cf_%' ORDER BY name"
+      "SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table' AND name NOT LIKE 'sqlite_%' AND name NOT GLOB '_cf_*' ORDER BY name"
```
</details>

<!-- suggestion_start -->

<summary>📝 Committable suggestion</summary>

> Carefully review the code before committing. Ensure that it accurately replaces the highlighted code, contains no missing lines, and has no issues with indentation. Thoroughly test & benchmark the code to ensure it meets the requirements.

```

Missing error handling: The tool handlers don't catch errors from the underlying D1 functions. If a tool execution fails (e.g., invalid SQL, table not found), the error will propagate unhandled to the MCP SDK. Consider wrapping the tool implementations in try-catch blocks to provide better error messages to the MCP client. For example:
```typescript
async () => {
  try {
    return {
      content: [{ type: "text", text: JSON.stringify(await listTables(env.DB), null, 2) }]
    };
  } catch (error) {
    return {
      content: [{ type: "text", text: `Error: ${error.message}` }],
      isError: true
    };
  }
}
```

        changes: result.meta?.changes ?? 0,
        last_row_id: result.meta?.last_row_id ?? 0,
        duration: result.meta?.duration ?? 0,
      },
    };
  } catch (error) {
    const message = error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error);
    throw new Error(`Failed to execute D1 query: ${message}`);
  }
```
---
```

     1→export async function listTables(db: D1Database): Promise<string[]> {
     2→  const result = await db
     3→    .prepare(
     4→      "SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table' AND name NOT LIKE 'sqlite_%' AND name NOT LIKE '_cf_%' ORDER BY name"
     5→    )
     6→    .all<{ name: string }>();
     7→  return result.results.map((row) => row.name);
     8→}
     9→
    10→export interface ColumnInfo {
    11→  cid: number;
    12→  name: string;
    13→  type: string;
    14→  notnull: number;
    15→  dflt_value: string | null;
    16→  pk: number;
    17→}
    18→
    19→export async function describeTable(db: D1Database, table: string): Promise<ColumnInfo[]> {
    20→  if (!/^[a-zA-Z_][a-zA-Z0-9_]*$/.test(table)) {
    21→    throw new Error(`Invalid table name: ${table}`);
    22→  }
    23→  const result = await db.prepare(`PRAGMA table_info(${table})`).all<ColumnInfo>();
    24→  if (result.results.length === 0) {
    25→    throw new Error(`Table not found: ${table}`);
    26→  }
    27→  return result.results;
    28→}
    29→
    30→export interface QueryResult {
    31→  results: Record<string, unknown>[];
    32→  meta: {
    33→    changes: number;
    34→    last_row_id: number;
    35→    duration: number;
    36→  };
    37→}
    38→
    39→export async function queryD1(
    40→  db: D1Database,
    41→  sql: string,
    42→  params?: unknown[]
    43→): Promise<QueryResult> {
    44→  const stmt = db.prepare(sql);
    45→  const bound = params && params.length > 0 ? stmt.bind(...params) : stmt;
    46→  const result = await bound.all();
    47→  return {
    48→    results: result.results as Record<string, unknown>[],
    49→    meta: {
    50→      changes: result.meta?.changes ?? 0,
    51→      last_row_id: result.meta?.last_row_id ?? 0,
    52→      duration: result.meta?.duration ?? 0,
    53→    },
    54→  };
    55→}
    56→

  "old_string": "import type { Context, Next } from \"hono\";\nimport type { Bindings } from \"../types\";\n\nexport async function bearerAuth(c: Context<{ Bindings: Bindings }>, next: Next) {\n  if (c.req.path === \"/health\") {\n    return next();\n  }\n\n  const authHeader = c.req.header(\"Authorization\");\n  if (!authHeader?.startsWith(\"Bearer \")) {\n    return c.json({ error: \"Missing or invalid Authorization header\" }, 401);\n  }\n\n  const token = authHeader.slice(7);\n  if (token !== c.env.MCP_AUTH_TOKEN) {\n    return c.json({ error: \"Invalid token\" }, 401);\n  }\n\n  return next();\n}",
  "new_string": "import { timingSafeEqual } from \"hono/utils/crypto\";\nimport type { Context, Next } from \"hono\";\nimport type { Bindings } from \"../types\";\n\nexport async function bearerAuth(c: Context<{ Bindings: Bindings }>, next: Next) {\n  if (c.req.path === \"/health\") {\n    return next();\n  }\n\n  const authHeader = c.req.header(\"Authorization\");\n  if (!authHeader?.startsWith(\"Bearer \")) {\n    return c.json({ error: \"Missing or invalid Authorization header\" }, 401);\n  }\n\n  const token = authHeader.slice(7);\n  const expected = c.env.MCP_AUTH_TOKEN;\n  if (token.length !== expected.length || !(await timingSafeEqual(token, expected))) {\n    return c.json({ error: \"Invalid token\" }, 401);\n  }\n\n  return next();\n}"
}

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/ta93abe/Developer/github.com/ta93abe/data-engineering-with-cloudflare/mcp-server/src/middleware/auth.ts",
  "old_string": "import type { Context, Next } from \"hono\";\nimport { timingSafeEqual } from \"hono/utils/crypto\";\nimport type { Bindings } from \"../types\";\n\nexport async function bearerAuth(c: Context<{ Bindings: Bindings }>, next: Next) {\n  if (c.req.path === \"/health\") {\n    return next();\n  }\n\n  const authHeader = c.req.header(\"Authorization\");\n  if (!authHeader?.startsWith(\"Bearer \")) {\n    return c.json({ error: \"Missing or invalid Authorization header\" }, 401);\n  }\n\n  const token = authHeader.slice(7);\n  const expected = c.env.MCP_AUTH_TOKEN;\n  if (token.length !== expected.length || !(await timingSafeEqual(token, expected))) {\n    return c.json({ error: \"Invalid token\" }, 401);\n  }\n\n  return next();\n}",
  "new_string": "import type { Context, Next } from \"hono\";\nimport type { Bindings } from \"../types\";\n\nfunction timingSafeEqual(a: string, b: string): boolean {\n  if (a.length !== b.length) {\n    return false;\n  }\n  const encoder = new TextEncoder();\n  const bufA = encoder.encode(a);\n  const bufB = encoder.encode(b);\n  return crypto.subtle.timingSafeEqual(bufA, bufB);\n}\n\nexport async function bearerAuth(c: Context<{ Bindings: Bindings }>, next: Next) {\n  if (c.req.path === \"/health\") {\n    return next();\n  }\n\n  const authHeader = c.req.header(\"Authorization\");\n  if (!authHeader?.startsWith(\"Bearer \")) {\n    return c.json({ error: \"Missing or invalid Authorization header\" }, 401);\n  }\n\n  const token = authHeader.slice(7);\n  if (!timingSafeEqual(token, c.env.MCP_AUTH_TOKEN)) {\n    return c.json({ error: \"Invalid token\" }, 401);\n  }\n\n  return next();\n}"
}
